## CPF-FR-005003

- **Work Item / Priority:** `CPF-WP-BAT-CALL-ASYNC-03-VERIFICATION_EVIDENCE` / `P0`
- **Requirement / Acceptance:** [BAT-CALL-ASYNC / 기능 목적·업무 결과] Batch/Worker Event/Outbox 비동기 호출에 stable message ID·retry/DLT·consumer idempotency·completion correlation을 적용한다. 이를 위해 해결할 업무 문제, 사용 역할, 미제공 위험과 완료 결과를 정의한다. 선언·Interface·DTO·문서만 추가하지 말고 실제 Consumer와 전체 호출 경로를 구현한다. | Batch 개발자·운영자가 실제 제품 경로에서 기능 목적·업무 결과 기능을 사용할 때 Batch/Worker Event/Outbox 비동기 호출에 stable message ID·retry/DLT·consumer idempotency·completion correlation을 적용한다.의 결과와 상태·권한·Audit·복구 규칙이 일치해야 한다. Source/API/SQL/Test/Config/Frontend/Script와 exact-SHA Evidence가 연결돼야 한다.
- **Source / Default implementation:** cpf-batch/execution-runtime/src/main/java/com/cpf/batch/execution/CpfBatchKafkaRemoteConfiguration.java; cpf-batch/execution-runtime/src/main/java/com/cpf/batch/execution/CpfBatchKafkaInboundBridge.java; cpf-batch/execution-runtime/src/main/java/com/cpf/batch/execution/CpfBatchKafkaManagerListener.java; cpf-batch/execution-runtime/src/main/java/com/cpf/batch/execution/CpfBatchKafkaWorkerListener.java; cpf-batch/execution-runtime/src/main/java/com/cpf/batch/execution/CpfKafkaOutboundMessageChannel.java; cpf-batch/execution-runtime/src/main/java/com/cpf/batch/execution/JdbcCpfBatchRemoteMessageLedger.java | BAT-CALL-ASYNC product implementation traced at fc207ac5560da59f352ee0c5f83199177f2987b4
- **Consumer / Full call path:** Job·Scheduler·Worker·ADM | Manager/worker Spring Batch steps and Kafka listeners | Manager -> canonical envelope/outbound channel -> Kafka -> worker listener/handler -> result topic -> manager listener -> message ledger/step completion
- **Normal / Error / Partial failure:** Correlated request/result envelope is durably deduplicated and ACKed at the correct boundary. | Decode failure, duplicate, late response, ACK loss and dependency outage preserve UNKNOWN/DLQ evidence. | invalid·401·403·404·409·429·timeout·dependency failure 표준 분류

- **Idempotency / Concurrency / Security:** operationId+requestHash·CAS/lease/fencing 중 적용 | retry=안전한 단계만 bounded backoff+jitter; blind retry 금지 | Payload validation, topic/consumer isolation and sanitized error evidence. | authN/authZ·trust boundary·Secret 외부화·secure default | Resource·Action·API·Field·Command 권한 Server 강제 | actor·target·before/after·reason·approval·result·transactionId append-only
